static std::string json_string(std::string_view s) {
    std::string r="\"";
    for(unsigned char c:s) {
        if(c=='"'||c=='\\'){r+='\\';r+=char(c);}
        else if(c=='\n')r+="\\n";
        else if(c=='\r')r+="\\r";
        else if(c=='\t')r+="\\t";
        else {require(c>=32,"control byte in JSON");r+=char(c);}
    }
    return r+'"';
}
static std::string lean_term(const ExpressionGraph& graph,int32_t root) {
    size_t fuel=30000;std::map<uint32_t,size_t> bindings;
    std::function<std::string(int32_t,size_t)> emit=[&](int32_t id,size_t depth)->std::string {
        require(fuel>0&&depth<128,"Lean translation budget");--fuel;const auto& n=graph.at(id);
        auto sub=[&](int32_t child){return emit(child,depth+1);};std::string r;
        switch(n.kind) {
        case ExprKind::Constant:r="(.value (("+n.value.get_num().get_str()+") / "+n.value.get_den().get_str()+" : ℚ))";break;
        case ExprKind::Variable:r="(.value (_x "+std::to_string(n.index)+"))";break;
        case ExprKind::Add:r="(.add "+sub(n.left)+" "+sub(n.right)+")";break;
        case ExprKind::Multiply:r="(.mul "+sub(n.left)+" "+sub(n.right)+")";break;
        case ExprKind::Negate:r="(.neg "+sub(n.left)+")";break;
        case ExprKind::Power:r="(.pow "+sub(n.left)+" "+std::to_string(n.index)+")";break;
        case ExprKind::IfLess:r="(.ifLess "+sub(n.guard_left)+" "+sub(n.guard_right)+" "+sub(n.left)+" "+sub(n.right)+")";break;
        case ExprKind::Tensor:
            r="(.tensor ![";for(size_t i=0;i<n.entries.size();++i)r+=(i?", ":"")+sub(n.entries[i]);r+="])";break;
        case ExprKind::BoundedLoop: {
            auto initial=sub(n.left);auto tag=std::to_string(bindings.size());bindings[n.binding]=bindings.size();auto body=sub(n.right);bindings.erase(n.binding);
            r="(.loop "+std::to_string(n.index)+" "+initial+" (fun _j"+tag+" _s"+tag+" => "+body+"))";break;
        }
        case ExprKind::Accumulator:case ExprKind::LoopIndex:
            require(bindings.count(n.binding),"unbound local in Lean translation");
            r=n.kind==ExprKind::Accumulator?"(.value _s"+std::to_string(bindings.at(n.binding))+")":
                "(.value (_j"+std::to_string(bindings.at(n.binding))+" : ℚ))";break;
        }
        require(r.size()<1024*1024,"Lean output budget");return r;
    };return emit(root,0);
}
static void ensure_directory(const std::string& path) {
    require(!path.empty()&&path.size()<PATH_MAX,"invalid output directory");
    std::string part;
    for(size_t i=0;i<=path.size();++i) {
        if(i<path.size())part+=path[i];
        if((i==path.size()||path[i]=='/')&&!part.empty()) {
            require(::mkdir(part.c_str(),0700)==0||errno==EEXIST,"cannot create output directory");
        }
    }
    struct stat st{};require(::stat(path.c_str(),&st)==0&&S_ISDIR(st.st_mode),"output is not a directory");
}
// An unsimplified scalar projection for each output cell. `change` in the
// generated proof requires Lean's kernel to confirm definitional equality with
// Term.denote; this translation is never accepted merely on the C++ checker's word.
static std::vector<std::string> lean_projections(const ExpressionGraph& graph,int32_t root) {
    using Cells=std::vector<std::string>;
    std::map<uint32_t,std::pair<Cells,uint32_t>> bindings;size_t fuel=50000;
    std::function<Cells(int32_t,size_t)> emit=[&](int32_t id,size_t depth)->Cells {
        require(fuel>0&&depth<128,"Lean projection budget");--fuel;const auto& n=graph.at(id);Cells r;
        switch(n.kind) {
        case ExprKind::Constant:return {"(("+n.value.get_num().get_str()+") / "+n.value.get_den().get_str()+" : ℚ)"};
        case ExprKind::Variable:return {"(x "+std::to_string(n.index)+")"};
        case ExprKind::Accumulator:return bindings.at(n.binding).first;
        case ExprKind::LoopIndex:return {"("+std::to_string(bindings.at(n.binding).second)+" : ℚ)"};
        case ExprKind::Tensor:for(auto e:n.entries)r.push_back(emit(e,depth+1).at(0));break;
        case ExprKind::BoundedLoop:
            r=emit(n.left,depth+1);
            for(uint32_t i=0;i<n.index;++i){bindings[n.binding]={r,i};r=emit(n.right,depth+1);}
            bindings.erase(n.binding);break;
        case ExprKind::IfLess: {
            auto a=emit(n.guard_left,depth+1).at(0),b=emit(n.guard_right,depth+1).at(0);
            auto yes=emit(n.left,depth+1),no=emit(n.right,depth+1);
            for(size_t i=0;i<yes.size();++i)r.push_back("(if "+a+" < "+b+" then "+yes[i]+" else "+no[i]+")");
            break;
        }
        default: {
            auto a=emit(n.left,depth+1);Cells b;if(n.right>=0)b=emit(n.right,depth+1);
            for(size_t i=0;i<a.size();++i) {
                if(n.kind==ExprKind::Add)r.push_back("("+a[i]+" + "+b[i]+")");
                else if(n.kind==ExprKind::Multiply)r.push_back("("+a[i]+" * "+b[i]+")");
                else if(n.kind==ExprKind::Negate)r.push_back("(-"+a[i]+")");
                else r.push_back("("+a[i]+" ^ "+std::to_string(n.index)+")");
            }
        }
        }
        size_t size=0;for(const auto& s:r)size+=s.size();require(size<1024*1024,"unrolled Lean projection budget");return r;
    };return emit(root,0);
}
static std::string export_lean(TrustedKernel& kernel,ExpressionGraph& graph,int32_t before,int32_t after,
    size_t vars,const ProofCertificate& claimed,const std::string& directory) {
    // Never trust a bool or a caller-provided digest as proof evidence.
    auto checked=certify_graph_improvement(kernel,graph,before,after,vars);
    require(checked.statement_hash==claimed.statement_hash&&checked.proof_hash==claimed.proof_hash&&
        checked.evidence==claimed.evidence,"certificate/AST mismatch at export");
    ensure_directory(directory);auto tag=checked.statement_hash;
    auto path=directory+"/Rewrite_"+tag+".lean";
    std::string type="ℚ";const auto& shape=graph.at(before).shape;
    if(!shape.empty()) {
        type="Fin "+std::to_string(ExpressionGraph::elements(shape))+" → ℚ";
    }
    std::string text="module\npublic import LeanML.Programs.VerifiedRewrite\n@[expose] public section\n";
    text+="noncomputable section\nopen LeanML.Programs\nset_option maxRecDepth 4096\nset_option maxHeartbeats 2000000\n-- Generated fallback branches may be unused for a particular expression.\nset_option linter.unusedTactic false\nset_option linter.unreachableTactic false\nset_option linter.unusedSimpArgs false\n";
    text+="namespace LeanML.Generated.Cert_"+tag+"\n";
    text+="-- BLAKE3 statement: "+tag+"\n-- BLAKE3 C++ proof evidence: "+checked.proof_hash+"\n";
    text+="-- C++ equivalence has been checked; this theorem still requires the Lean kernel.\n";
    if(!shape.empty()) {
        text+="def outputShape : List ℕ := [";
        for(size_t i=0;i<shape.size();++i)text+=(i?", ":"")+std::to_string(shape[i]);
        text+="]\n";
        text+="theorem shape_size : outputShape.prod = "+std::to_string(ExpressionGraph::elements(shape))+" := by decide\n";
    }
    text+="def before (_x : Fin "+std::to_string(vars)+" → ℚ) : Term ("+type+") := "+lean_term(graph,before)+"\n";
    text+="def after (_x : Fin "+std::to_string(vars)+" → ℚ) : Term ("+type+") := "+lean_term(graph,after)+"\n";
    text+="theorem certified : VerifiedRewrite before after where\n  equivalent x := by\n";
    auto lhs=lean_projections(graph,before),rhs=lean_projections(graph,after);
    if(!shape.empty())text+="    funext i\n    fin_cases i\n";
    for(size_t i=0;i<lhs.size();++i) {
        auto indent=shape.empty()?"    ":"    · ";
        text+=indent+std::string("simp only [before, after, Term.denote, boundedLoop, ite_apply, Pi.add_apply, Pi.mul_apply, Pi.neg_apply, Pi.pow_apply]\n");
        auto next=shape.empty()?"    ":"      ";
        text+=next+std::string("all_goals try dsimp +instances only [Term.denote]\n");
        text+=next+std::string("all_goals try rw [ite_apply]\n");
        text+=next+std::string("all_goals change ")+lhs[i]+" = "+rhs[i]+"\n";
        text+=next+std::string("all_goals first | (ring; done) | (split_ifs; all_goals norm_num at *; all_goals ring)\n");
    }
    text+="  cheaper x := by\n    change "+std::to_string(checked.new_static_cost)+" < "+std::to_string(checked.old_static_cost)+"\n    decide\n";
    text+="end LeanML.Generated.Cert_"+tag+"\n";
    require(atomic_write(path,text),"cannot atomically write Lean certificate");
    std::string metadata="{\n  \"schema\": \"godel-leanml-rewrite-v2\",\n  \"statement_blake3\": "+json_string(tag)+
        ",\n  \"proof_blake3\": "+json_string(checked.proof_hash)+",\n  \"evidence\": "+json_string(checked.evidence)+
        ",\n  \"cpp_verified\": true,\n  \"lean_status\": \"pending-kernel\",\n  \"lean_file\": "+json_string(path)+"\n}\n";
    require(atomic_write(path+".json",metadata),"cannot write certificate metadata");return path;
}
static std::string export_ast_certificate(const ProofCertificate& cert,const std::string& directory) {
    std::string_view rest=cert.evidence,prefix,vars,before,after,oldcost,newcost,ring_hash;
    require(split_once(rest,'|',prefix,rest)&&(prefix=="ASTv1"||prefix=="ASTv2"),"unsupported export certificate");
    require(split_once(rest,'|',vars,rest)&&split_once(rest,'|',before,rest)&&split_once(rest,'|',after,rest)&&
        split_once(rest,'|',oldcost,rest)&&split_once(rest,'|',newcost,ring_hash),"invalid export evidence");
    size_t n=parse_u32(vars);ExpressionGraph graph;TrustedKernel kernel;
    auto a=AlgebraParser(graph,before,n).parse(),b=AlgebraParser(graph,after,n).parse();
    return export_lean(kernel,graph,a,b,n,cert,directory);
}
===== FIM DO ARQUIVO: tools/godel/lean_export.hpp =====

===== INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: tools/godel/program_parser.hpp =====
class AlgebraParser {
    ExpressionGraph& graph_;
    std::string_view input_;
    size_t offset_=0,variables_=0,operations_=0,depth_=0;
    std::vector<std::pair<int32_t,int32_t>> bindings_;
    struct Depth {
        size_t& d;explicit Depth(size_t& v):d(v){require(++d<128,"parser nesting budget");}
        ~Depth(){--d;}
    };
    void space(){while(offset_<input_.size()&&(input_[offset_]==' '||input_[offset_]=='\t'))++offset_;}
    bool accept(char c){space();if(offset_<input_.size()&&input_[offset_]==c){++offset_;return true;}return false;}
    void expect(char c){require(accept(c),std::string("expected ")+c);}
    bool keyword(std::string_view word) {
        space();if(input_.substr(offset_,word.size())!=word)return false;
        offset_+=word.size();return true;
    }
    uint32_t natural() {
        space();size_t start=offset_;
        while(offset_<input_.size()&&std::isdigit((unsigned char)input_[offset_]))++offset_;
        require(offset_>start,"expected natural number");return parse_u32(input_.substr(start,offset_-start));
    }
    int32_t atom() {
        Depth guard(depth_);require(++operations_<20000,"parser operation budget");
        if(accept('(')){auto r=expression();expect(')');return r;}
        if(keyword("q")) {
            expect('(');space();size_t start=offset_;
            if(offset_<input_.size()&&input_[offset_]=='-')++offset_;
            size_t digits=offset_;
            while(offset_<input_.size()&&std::isdigit((unsigned char)input_[offset_]))++offset_;
            require(offset_>digits&&offset_-digits<=512,"invalid rational numerator");
            std::string value(input_.substr(start,offset_-start));
            if(accept('/')) {
                space();size_t ds=offset_;
                while(offset_<input_.size()&&std::isdigit((unsigned char)input_[offset_]))++offset_;
                require(offset_>ds&&offset_-ds<=512,"invalid rational denominator");
                auto den=std::string(input_.substr(ds,offset_-ds));require(Z(den)!=0,"zero denominator");value+="/"+den;
            }
            expect(')');return graph_.constant(Q(value));
        }
        if(keyword("iflt")) {
            expect('(');auto a=expression();expect(',');auto b=expression();expect(',');
            auto yes=expression();expect(',');auto no=expression();expect(')');return graph_.if_less(a,b,yes,no);
        }
        if(keyword("tensor")) {
            expect('(');expect('[');std::vector<uint32_t> shape;
            do{shape.push_back(natural());require(shape.size()<=4,"tensor rank budget");}while(accept(','));
            expect(']');expect(',');expect('[');std::vector<int32_t> cells;
            do{cells.push_back(expression());require(cells.size()<=1024,"tensor cell budget");}while(accept(','));
            expect(']');expect(')');return graph_.tensor(std::move(shape),std::move(cells));
        }
        if(keyword("matmul")) {
            expect('(');auto a=expression();expect(',');auto b=expression();expect(')');return graph_.matrix_multiply(a,b);
        }
        if(keyword("fold")) {
            expect('(');auto count=natural();expect(',');auto initial=expression();expect(',');
            auto r=graph_.bounded_loop(count,initial,[&](int32_t a,int32_t i) {
                bindings_.push_back({a,i});auto body=expression();bindings_.pop_back();return body;
            });
            expect(')');return r;
        }
        if(accept('x')){auto i=natural();require(i<variables_,"input variable out of range");return graph_.variable(i);}
        space();if(offset_<input_.size()&&(input_[offset_]=='a'||input_[offset_]=='i')) {
            bool acc=input_[offset_++]=='a';auto depth=natural();require(depth<bindings_.size(),"unbound loop variable");
            auto b=bindings_[bindings_.size()-1-depth];return acc?b.first:b.second;
        }
        space();size_t start=offset_;
        while(offset_<input_.size()&&std::isdigit((unsigned char)input_[offset_]))++offset_;
        require(offset_>start&&offset_-start<=512,"expected bounded integer");
        std::string number(input_.substr(start,offset_-start));
        if(accept('/')) {
            space();size_t ds=offset_;
            while(offset_<input_.size()&&std::isdigit((unsigned char)input_[offset_]))++offset_;
            require(offset_>ds&&offset_-ds<=512,"expected bounded denominator");
            auto den=std::string(input_.substr(ds,offset_-ds));require(Z(den)!=0,"zero denominator");number+="/"+den;
        }
        Q q(number);q.canonicalize();return graph_.constant(q);
    }
    int32_t unary(){Depth guard(depth_);if(accept('-'))return graph_.negate(unary());return atom();}
    int32_t power(){auto r=unary();if(accept('^'))r=graph_.power(r,natural());return r;}
    int32_t product(){auto r=power();while(accept('*'))r=graph_.multiply(r,power());return r;}
    int32_t expression(){auto r=product();for(;;) {
        if(accept('+'))r=graph_.add(r,product());
        else if(accept('-'))r=graph_.add(r,graph_.negate(product()));else return r;
    }}
public:
    AlgebraParser(ExpressionGraph& g,std::string_view text,size_t vars):graph_(g),input_(text),variables_(vars) {
        require(text.size()<100000&&vars>0&&vars<=16,"program input budget");
    }
    int32_t parse(){auto r=expression();space();require(offset_==input_.size(),"trailing program input");return r;}
};
===== FIM DO ARQUIVO: tools/godel/program_parser.hpp =====

===== INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: tools/godel/propose.py =====
#!/usr/bin/env python3
"""Untrusted POSIX-shm proposal client. Models return DSL, never C++ or Lean proofs."""
import argparse
import contextlib
import fcntl
import json
import mmap
import os
from pathlib import Path
import re
import stat
import struct
import subprocess
import time

HEADER = struct.Struct('<8sIIQII')
CAPACITY = 1024 * 1024


@contextlib.contextmanager
def locked(fd, deadline):
    while True:
        try:
            fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_EX | fcntl.LOCK_NB)
            break
        except BlockingIOError:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise TimeoutError('IPC lock timeout')
            time.sleep(.002)
    try:
        yield
    finally:
        fcntl.flock(fd, fcntl.LOCK_UN)


def exchange(name, propose, timeout=60):
    """Read one request, call propose(request_dict), submit DSL, return verdict."""
    if not re.fullmatch(r'/[A-Za-z0-9_-]{1,118}', name):
        raise ValueError('invalid shm name')
    deadline = time.monotonic() + timeout
    path = Path('/dev/shm') / name[1:]
    while True:
        try:
            fd = os.open(path, os.O_RDWR | os.O_CLOEXEC | os.O_NOFOLLOW)
            break
        except FileNotFoundError:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise TimeoutError('mailbox did not appear')
            time.sleep(.002)
    try:
        info = os.fstat(fd)
        if info.st_uid != os.getuid() or info.st_mode & 0o077 or not stat.S_ISREG(info.st_mode):
            raise ValueError('mailbox must be a private file owned by this user')
        # The owner sizes/initializes the mapping immediately after exclusive creation.
        while os.fstat(fd).st_size != HEADER.size + CAPACITY:
            if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                raise TimeoutError('mailbox initialization timeout')
            time.sleep(.002)
        with mmap.mmap(fd, HEADER.size + CAPACITY) as shared:
            def read():
                magic, version, state, request_id, size, reserved = HEADER.unpack_from(shared)
                if magic != b'GDIPC001' or version != 1 or reserved != 0 or size > CAPACITY:
                    raise ValueError('invalid IPC header')
                return state, request_id, shared[HEADER.size:HEADER.size + size]

            while True:
                with locked(fd, deadline):
                    state, request_id, payload = read()
                    if state == 1:
                        request = json.loads(payload)
                        if request['request_id'] != str(request_id):
                            raise ValueError('request id/header mismatch')
                        break
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    raise TimeoutError('no request')
                time.sleep(.002)
            replacement = propose(request)
            if not isinstance(replacement, str) or '\n' in replacement or '\r' in replacement:
                raise ValueError('model must return one DSL expression on one line')
            response = (f'GODEL_REPLACE_V1\n{request_id}\n{request["source_blake3"]}\n{replacement}').encode()
            if len(response) >= 100000:
                raise ValueError('proposal exceeds parser budget')
            with locked(fd, deadline):
                state, current_id, _ = read()
                if state != 1 or current_id != request_id:
                    raise ValueError('request changed while model was reasoning')
                shared[HEADER.size:HEADER.size+len(response)] = response
                HEADER.pack_into(shared, 0, b'GDIPC001', 1, 2, request_id, len(response), 0)
            while True:
                with locked(fd, deadline):
                    state, current_id, payload = read()
                    if current_id != request_id:
                        raise ValueError('stale result')
                    if state == 3:
                        return json.loads(payload)
                if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
                    raise TimeoutError('verification result timeout')
                time.sleep(.002)
    finally:
        os.close(fd)


def main():
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=__doc__)
    parser.add_argument('--shm', required=True)
    parser.add_argument('--timeout', type=float, default=60)
    group = parser.add_mutually_exclusive_group(required=True)
    group.add_argument('--replacement', help='literal DSL proposal for manual tests')
    group.add_argument('--command', nargs=argparse.REMAINDER,
                       help='external model command: JSON request on stdin, DSL on stdout')
    args = parser.parse_args()

    def propose(request):
        if args.replacement is not None:
            return args.replacement
        if not args.command:
            raise ValueError('--command needs an executable')
        result = subprocess.run(args.command, input=json.dumps(request), text=True,
                                capture_output=True, timeout=args.timeout, check=True)
        return result.stdout.strip()

    result = exchange(args.shm, propose, args.timeout)
    print(json.dumps(result, ensure_ascii=False))
    return 0 if result['accepted'] else 1


if __name__ == '__main__':
    raise SystemExit(main())
===== FIM DO ARQUIVO: tools/godel/propose.py =====

===== INÍCIO DO ARQUIVO: tools/godel/shared_memory.hpp =====
// POSIX single-flight mailbox. All readers/writers take flock on the shm fd.
// Wire integers are little-endian; no std::atomic ABI is shared with clients.
class SharedMemoryMailbox {
    int fd_=-1;uint8_t* bytes_=nullptr;std::string name_;
    static constexpr size_t header_size=32,capacity=1024*1024;
    uint64_t get(size_t pos,size_t width)const {
        uint64_t r=0;for(size_t i=0;i<width;++i)r|=uint64_t(bytes_[pos+i])<<(8*i);return r;
    }
    void put(size_t pos,size_t width,uint64_t n) {
        for(size_t i=0;i<width;++i)bytes_[pos+i]=(uint8_t)(n>>(8*i));
    }
    void lock(Clock::time_point deadline) {
        while(::flock(fd_,LOCK_EX|LOCK_NB)!=0) {
            require(errno==EAGAIN||errno==EWOULDBLOCK||errno==EINTR,"IPC lock failure");
            require(Clock::now()<deadline,"IPC lock timeout");::usleep(1000);
        }
    }
    void unlock(){::flock(fd_,LOCK_UN);}
    void validate()const {
        require(std::memcmp(bytes_,"GDIPC001",8)==0&&get(8,4)==1&&get(28,4)==0,"IPC schema mismatch");
        require(get(24,4)<=capacity,"IPC payload length exceeds capacity");
    }
public:
    explicit SharedMemoryMailbox(std::string name):name_(std::move(name)) {
        require(name_.size()>1&&name_.size()<120&&name_[0]=='/',"invalid POSIX shm name");
        for(size_t i=1;i<name_.size();++i)require(std::isalnum((unsigned char)name_[i])||name_[i]=='_'||name_[i]=='-',"invalid shm name character");
        fd_=::shm_open(name_.c_str(),O_RDWR|O_CREAT|O_EXCL|O_CLOEXEC,0600);
        require(fd_>=0,"cannot create exclusive IPC mailbox (name already in use?)");
        try {
            lock(Clock::now()+std::chrono::milliseconds(1000));
            require(::fchmod(fd_,0600)==0&&::ftruncate(fd_,header_size+capacity)==0,"cannot size IPC mailbox");
            void* p=::mmap(nullptr,header_size+capacity,PROT_READ|PROT_WRITE,MAP_SHARED,fd_,0);
            require(p!=MAP_FAILED,"cannot map IPC mailbox");bytes_=static_cast<uint8_t*>(p);
            std::memset(bytes_,0,header_size);std::memcpy(bytes_,"GDIPC001",8);put(8,4,1);unlock();
        }catch(...) {::close(fd_);fd_=-1;::shm_unlink(name_.c_str());throw;}
    }
    SharedMemoryMailbox(const SharedMemoryMailbox&)=delete;
    ~SharedMemoryMailbox() {
        if(bytes_)::munmap(bytes_,header_size+capacity);
        if(fd_>=0){::close(fd_);::shm_unlink(name_.c_str());}
    }
    void publish(uint32_t state,uint64_t request,std::string_view payload,Clock::time_point deadline) {
        require(payload.size()<=capacity,"outgoing IPC payload budget");lock(deadline);
        std::memcpy(bytes_+header_size,payload.data(),payload.size());
        put(16,8,request);put(24,4,payload.size());put(12,4,state);unlock();
    }
    std::string response(uint64_t request,Clock::time_point deadline) {
        while(Clock::now()<deadline) {
            lock(deadline);
            try {
                validate();auto state=get(12,4);
                require(get(16,8)==request,"IPC stale request id");
                if(state==2) {
                    std::string r(reinterpret_cast<char*>(bytes_+header_size),(size_t)get(24,4));unlock();return r;
                }
                require(state==1,"unexpected IPC state");unlock();
            }catch(...) {unlock();throw;}
            ::usleep(2000);
        }
        throw std::runtime_error("IPC proposal timeout");
    }
};
static std::string proposal_expression(std::string_view response,uint64_t request,const std::string& source_hash) {
    require(response.size()<100000&&response.find('\0')==std::string_view::npos,"IPC response size/encoding");
    std::string_view magic,id,hash,expression,rest;
    require(split_once(response,'\n',magic,rest)&&magic=="GODEL_REPLACE_V1"&&
        split_once(rest,'\n',id,rest)&&split_once(rest,'\n',hash,expression),"invalid proposal frame");
    require(id==std::to_string(request)&&hash==source_hash,"proposal is bound to another request/source");
    require(expression.find('\n')==std::string_view::npos&&expression.find('\r')==std::string_view::npos,"proposal must contain exactly one expression");
    return std::string(expression);
}
static int ipc_optimize(const std::string& name,size_t vars,const std::string& source,
    uint32_t timeout_ms,const std::string& output) {
    require(timeout_ms>0&&timeout_ms<=60000,"IPC timeout range 1..60000 ms");
    ExpressionGraph graph;TrustedKernel kernel;auto before=AlgebraParser(graph,source,vars).parse();
    // Validate all input semantics before publishing a request.
    graph.symbolic(before,vars);auto repr=graph.repr(before),hash=blake3::hex(repr);
    SharedMemoryMailbox mailbox(name);
    uint64_t id=uint64_t(Clock::now().time_since_epoch().count())^uint64_t(::getpid());
    auto deadline=Clock::now()+std::chrono::milliseconds(timeout_ms);
    std::string request="{\"schema\":\"godel-proposal-v1\",\"request_id\":\""+std::to_string(id)+
        "\",\"variables\":"+std::to_string(vars)+",\"source_blake3\":"+json_string(hash)+
        ",\"source\":"+json_string(repr)+",\"static_cost\":"+std::to_string(graph.static_cost(before))+"}";
    mailbox.publish(1,id,request,deadline);std::cout<<"[IPC] ready "<<name<<std::endl;
    try {
        auto text=proposal_expression(mailbox.response(id,deadline),id,hash);
        auto after=AlgebraParser(graph,text,vars).parse();
        auto cert=certify_graph_improvement(kernel,graph,before,after,vars);
        auto path=export_lean(kernel,graph,before,after,vars,cert,output);
        auto result="{\"accepted\":true,\"cpp_verified\":true,\"lean_status\":\"pending-kernel\",\"lean_file\":"+json_string(path)+"}";
        mailbox.publish(3,id,result,Clock::now()+std::chrono::milliseconds(1000));
        std::cout<<result<<"\n";return 0;
    }catch(const std::exception& e) {
        auto result="{\"accepted\":false,\"error\":"+json_string(e.what())+"}";
        try{mailbox.publish(3,id,result,Clock::now()+std::chrono::milliseconds(100));}catch(...){}
        std::cerr<<result<<"\n";return 1;
    }
}
